monetary loss not measured.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null}],"transitions":[{"id":"place-indian-flowbind-order-v1","name":"Place Indian Flowbind replenishment order","description":"On external review when Indian Supplier accepts orders, create a PO to the approximate target at Indian MOQ multiples if inventory position is below approximate reorder point. Later physical receipt requires a matching external arrival event; lead time is not generated.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nreturn position < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point && parameters.indian_moq > 0 && parameters.flowbind_target > position;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nconst amount = Math.ceil((parameters.flowbind_target - position) / parameters.indian_moq) * parameters.indian_moq;\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units + amount }],\n  IndianOpenFlowbindOrders: [{ units: amount }]\n};"},{"id":"flowbind-review-no-order-v1","name":"Complete Flowbind review without replenishment","description":"Consumes a review event when the inventory position is at or above the reorder point; leaves balances untouched.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units >= parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"flowbind-indian-unavailable-v1","name":"Indian Supplier cannot accept Flowbind order","description":"On a due review below reorder point, if Indian Supplier cannot accept orders the review ends without an Indian PO. The distinct urgent German request may occur separately on a below-500 monitoring event; acceptance and delivery are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"request-german-bridge-v1","name":"Request urgent German Flowbind bridge","description":"Consumes externally supplied urgent monitoring event when released stock is below 500 and Indian Supplier cannot accept an order. Emits a request only; acceptance and supply are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-urgent-event-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-bridge-request-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"return input.FlowbindBalance[0].released_units < parameters.german_bridge_threshold && parameters.german_bridge_units > 0;","transitionKernelCode":"return { GermanFlowbindTopupRequests: [{ units: parameters.german_bridge_units }] };"},{"id":"receive-indian-flowbind-v1","name":"Receive full Indian Flowbind order into quarantine","description":"On matched external physical-arrival event, closes one full Indian PO and removes its quantity from inventory position; preserves full lot in quarantine without increasing usable stock.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst arrival = input.IndianFlowbindArrival[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn po.order_id === arrival.order_id && po.units === arrival.units && balance.open_units >= po.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units - po.units }],\n  FlowbindInQuarantine: [{ units: po.units, order_id: po.order_id }]\n};"},{"id":"release-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Release full Flowbind lot after quality clearance","description":"On matching external quality-clearance result, consumes the entire quarantined lot and adds all its units to released usable stock in the planning balance.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityCleared[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units + lot.units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"}],"types":[{"id":"flowbind-balance-v1","name":"Flowbind planning balance","description":"Released usable stock and open-order quantity in consistent standard units; quarantine is - tool ledger_commit (call_ro2jV8CzEnc7jzyamYXEpYLi|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7ea1b76c87d2928094ec97a908f3): {"status":"recorded","commitId":"call_ro2jV8CzEnc7jzyamYXEpYLi|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7ea1b76c87d2928094ec97a908f3","revision":23,"notes":[{"address":"construction/n65","supersedes":"construction/n64"},{"address":"delivery/n66","supersedes":"delivery/n60"},{"address":"open-matters/n67"}]}

## User
Production needs both materials—one unit of each per unit of Sonic Flow. If either is short when the batch is due to start, the run waits. It doesn't start with a partial allocation. That waiting time is the delay we're trying to avoid, and it has a cost.

## Assistant
For a batch due to start, is its Sonic Flow quantity already fixed, and are the full matching amounts of both materials committed at the start—or drawn down as production runs?
- tool ledger_commit (call_MBFe6pOcjetBQmKgZ3MDRv9v|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7ebbf2c887d2820a7f92ddad06d5): {"status":"recorded","commitId":"call_MBFe6pOcjetBQmKgZ3MDRv9v|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7ebbf2c887d2820a7f92ddad06d5","revision":24,"notes":[{"address":"operational/activities/n68"},{"address":"operational/process-spine/n69"},{"address":"operational/goals/n70"},{"address":"open-matters/n71"}]}

## User
The batch quantity is set when the production order is planned. Once the run starts the materials are consumed. The check is whether we have enough of both before we start—if we do, the run proceeds and takes about a week to complete.

## Assistant
When a batch is waiting for material, does it start as soon as both materials become available, or must it be rescheduled? And for your delay measure, is the clock from its original planned start to its actual start?

One token means Indian Supplier can accept an order; its outage and restoration are not generated here.","colorId":null,"dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null,"capacity":1},{"id":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","name":"IndianOpenFlowbindOrders","description":"Indian Supplier Flowbind POs whose full quantity remains open until a matched external arrival event closes the order and sends its lot to quarantine.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"german-urgent-event-v1","name":"GermanUrgentThresholdEvent","description":"Externally supplied event from ongoing monitoring of released stock; no review meeting needed. Generation/deduplication is outside the partial net.","colorId":null,"dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"german-bridge-request-v1","name":"GermanFlowbindTopupRequests","description":"Requests for an approximately 250-unit German bridge; not treated as accepted orders or usable supply.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","name":"IndianFlowbindArrival","description":"External physical arrival signal carrying the matching order identity and full ordered quantity; timing is not yet generated.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","name":"FlowbindInQuarantine","description":"Full received Flowbind lots excluded from usable stock and from inventory position until quality disposition.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","name":"FlowbindQualityCleared","description":"Externally supplied matching signal that quality cleared the entire quarantined lot; no completion timing is generated.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-quality-failed-v1","name":"FlowbindQualityRejected","description":"Externally supplied matching signal that quality rejected the entire quarantined lot; no failure probability or duration is inferred.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-disposed-v1","name":"FlowbindDisposed","description":"Rejected full Flowbind lots sent to disposal, preserving standard-unit amounts; monetary loss not measured.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null}],"transitions":[{"id":"place-indian-flowbind-order-v1","name":"Place Indian Flowbind replenishment order","description":"On external review when Indian Supplier accepts orders, create a PO to the approximate target at Indian MOQ multiples if inventory position is below approximate reorder point. Later physical receipt requires a matching external arrival event; lead time is not generated.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nreturn position < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point && parameters.indian_moq > 0 && parameters.flowbind_target > position;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nconst amount = Math.ceil((parameters.flowbind_target - position) / parameters.indian_moq) * parameters.indian_moq;\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units + amount }],\n  IndianOpenFlowbindOrders: [{ units: amount }]\n};"},{"id":"flowbind-review-no-order-v1","name":"Complete Flowbind review without replenishment","description":"Consumes a review event when the inventory position is at or above the reorder point; leaves balances untouched.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units >= parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"flowbind-indian-unavailable-v1","name":"Indian Supplier cannot accept Flowbind order","description":"On a due review below reorder point, if Indian Supplier cannot accept orders the review ends without an Indian PO. The distinct urgent German request may occur separately on a below-500 monitoring event; acceptance and delivery are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"request-german-bridge-v1","name":"Request urgent German Flowbind bridge","description":"Consumes externally supplied urgent monitoring event when released stock is below 500 and Indian Supplier cannot accept an order. Emits a request only; acceptance and supply are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-urgent-event-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-bridge-request-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"return input.FlowbindBalance[0].released_units < parameters.german_bridge_threshold && parameters.german_bridge_units > 0;","transitionKernelCode":"return { GermanFlowbindTopupRequests: [{ units: parameters.german_bridge_units }] };"},{"id":"receive-indian-flowbind-v1","name":"Receive full Indian Flowbind order into quarantine","description":"On matched external physical-arrival event, closes one full Indian PO and removes its quantity from inventory position; preserves full lot in quarantine without increasing usable stock.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst arrival = input.IndianFlowbindArrival[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn po.order_id === arrival.order_id && po.units === arrival.units && balance.open_units >= po.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units - po.units }],\n  FlowbindInQuarantine: [{ units: po.units, order_id: po.order_id }]\n};"},{"id":"release-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Release full Flowbind lot after quality clearance","description":"On matching external quality-clearance result, consumes the entire quarantined lot and adds all its units to released usable stock in the planning balance.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityCleared[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units + lot.units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"reject-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Reject and dispose of full Flowbind lot","description":"On matching external failed quality result, consumes the whole quarantined lot and records its quantity as disposed, not released or returned to supplier.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-failed-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-disposed-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityRejected[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nreturn { FlowbindDisposed: [{ units: lot.units, order_id: lot.order_id }] };"}],"types":[{"id":"flowbind-balance-v1","name":"Flowbind planning balance","description":"Released usable stock and open-order quantity in consistent standard units; quarantine is excluded.","elements":[{"elementId":"released-units-v1","name":"released_units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"open-units-v1","name":"open_units","type":"integer"}]},{"id":"flowbind-order-v1","name":"Flowbind usable stock","elements":[{"elementId":"sonaflozin-available-units-v1","name":"usable_units","type":"integer"}]}],"differentialEquations":[],"parameters":[{"id":"flowbind-reorder-v1","name":"Flowbind reorder point (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_reorder_point","type":"integer","defaultValue":"1500"},{"id":"flowbind-target-v1","name":"Flowbind target (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_target","type":"integer","defaultValue":"5000"},{"id":"indian-moq-v1","name":"Indian Supplier Flowbind ## User
It starts as soon as both materials are available. The delay is measured from when it was planned to start—that's the number we track.

## Assistant
How does Flowbind expiry affect what purchasing can count on? For a lot approaching expiry, when does it stop being usable for a Sonic Flow batch, and what happens to any units left then?

not treated as accepted orders or usable supply.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","name":"IndianFlowbindArrival","description":"External physical arrival signal carrying the matching order identity and full ordered quantity; no failure probability or duration is inferred.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-disposed-v1","name":"FlowbindDisposed","description":"Rejected full Flowbind lots sent to disposal, preserving standard-unit amounts; scheduling and arrival of new orders are not generated.","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"sonic-flow-in-progress-v1","name":"SonicFlowInProgress","description":"Batches started after consuming both full material quantities; completion duration and finished output are not yet represented.","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null}],"transitions":[{"id":"place-indian-flowbind-order-v1","name":"Place Indian Flowbind replenishment order","description":"On external review when Indian Supplier accepts orders, create a PO to the approximate target at Indian MOQ multiples if inventory position is below approximate reorder point. Later physical receipt requires a matching external arrival event; lead time is not generated.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nreturn position < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point && parameters.indian_moq > 0 && parameters.flowbind_target > position;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nconst amount = Math.ceil((parameters.flowbind_target - position) / parameters.indian_moq) * parameters.indian_moq;\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units + amount }],\n  IndianOpenFlowbindOrders: [{ units: amount }]\n};"},{"id":"flowbind-review-no-order-v1","name":"Complete Flowbind review without replenishment","description":"Consumes a review event when the inventory position is at or above the reorder point; leaves balances untouched.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units >= parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"flowbind-indian-unavailable-v1","name":"Indian Supplier cannot accept Flowbind order","description":"On a due review below reorder point, if Indian Supplier cannot accept orders the review ends without an Indian PO. input.FlowbindBalance[0].released_units < parameters.german_bridge_threshold && parameters.german_bridge_units > 0;","transitionKernelCode":"return { GermanFlowbindTopupRequests: [{ units: parameters.german_bridge_units }] };"},{"id":"receive-indian-flowbind-v1","name":"Receive full Indian Flowbind order into quarantine","description":"On matched external physical-arrival event, closes stock.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst arrival = input.IndianFlowbindArrival[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn po.order_id === arrival.order_id && po.units === arrival.units && balance.open_units >= po.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ }]\n};"},{"id":"release-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Release full Flowbind lot after quality clearance","description":"On matching external quality-clearance result, consumes the entire quarantined input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units + lot.units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"reject-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Reject and dispose of full Flowbind lot","description":"On matching external failed quality result, consumes the whole quarantined lot and records its Later physical receipt requires a matching external arrival event; lead time is not generated.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nreturn position < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point && parameters.indian_moq > 0 && parameters.flowbind_target > position;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nconst amount = Math.ceil((parameters.flowbind_target - position) / parameters.indian_moq) * parameters.indian_moq;\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units + amount }],\n  IndianOpenFlowbindOrders: [{ units: amount }]\n};"},{"id":"flowbind-review-no-order-v1","name":"Complete Flowbind review without replenishment","description":"Consumes a review event when the inventory position is at or above the reorder point; leaves balances untouched.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units >= parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"flowbind-indian-unavailable-v1","name":"Indian Supplier cannot accept Flowbind order","description":"On a due review below reorder point, if Indian Supplier cannot accept orders the review ends without an Indian PO. The distinct urgent German request may occur separately on a below-500 monitoring event; acceptance and delivery are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"request-german-bridge-v1","name":"Request urgent German Flowbind bridge","description":"Consumes externally supplied urgent monitoring event when released stock is below 500 and Indian Supplier cannot accept an order. Emits a request only; acceptance and supply are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-urgent-event-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-bridge-request-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"return input.FlowbindBalance[0].released_units < parameters.german_bridge_threshold && parameters.german_bridge_units > 0;","transitionKernelCode":"return { GermanFlowbindTopupRequests: [{ units: parameters.german_bridge_units }] };"},{"id":"receive-indian-flowbind-v1","name":"Receive full Indian Flowbind order into quarantine","description":"On matched external physical-arrival event, closes one full Indian PO and removes its quantity from inventory position; preserves full lot in quarantine without increasing usable stock.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst arrival = input.IndianFlowbindArrival[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn po.order_id === arrival.order_id && po.units === arrival.units && balance.open_units >= po.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units - po.units }],\n  FlowbindInQuarantine: [{ units: po.units, order_id: po.order_id }]\n};"},{"id":"release-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Release full Flowbind lot after quality clearance","description":"On matching external quality-clearance result, consumes the entire quarantined lot and adds all its units to released usable stock in the planning balance.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityCleared[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units + lot.units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"reject-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Reject and dispose of full Flowbind lot","description":"On matching external failed quality result, consumes the whole quarantined lot and records its quantity as disposed, not released or returned to supplier.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-failed-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-disposed-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityRejected[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nreturn { FlowbindDisposed: [{ units: lot.units, order_id: lot.order_id }] };"},{"id":"start-sonic-flow-v1","name":"Start full Sonic Flow production batch","description":"Starts only if enough released Flowbind and usable Sonaflozin exist for all planned units; consumes one of each per Sonic Flow unit and moves full batch into production. The wait and approximately week-long completion are not timed here.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"sonaflozin-usable-stock-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"sonic-flow-planned-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"sonaflozin-usable-stock-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"sonic-flow-in-progress-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const batch = input.SonicFlowPlannedBatches[0];\nconst flowbind = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst sonaflozin = input.SonaflozinUsableStock[0];\nreturn batch.units > 0 && flowbind.released_units >= batch.units && sonaflozin.usable_units >= batch.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const batch = input.SonicFlowPlannedBatches[0];\nconst flowbind = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst sonaflozin = input.SonaflozinUsableStock[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: flowbind.released_units - batch.units, open_units: flowbind.open_units }],\n  SonaflozinUsableStock: [{ usable_units: sonaflozin.usable_units - batch.units }],\n  SonicFlowInProgress: [{ units: batch.units, wait_seconds: batch.wait_seconds }]\n};"}],"types":[{"id":"flowbind-balance-v1","name":"Flowbind planning balance","description":"Released usable stock and open-order quantity in consistent standard units; quarantine is excluded.","elements":[{"elementId":"released-units-v1","name":"released_units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"open-units-v1","name":"open_units","type":"integer"}]},{"id":"flowbind-order-v1","name":"Flowbind purchase order","description":"One Flowbind order or received lot in standard units, with an identity for matching order, arrival and quarantine; German request is not yet an accepted order.","elements":[{"elementId":"ordered-units-v1","name":"units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"flowbind-order-identity-v1","name":"order_id","type":"uuid"}]},{"id":"sonaflozin-stock-type-v1","name":"Sonaflozin usable stock","elements":[{"elementId":"sonaflozin-available-units-v1","name":"usable_units","type":"integer"}]},{"id":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","name":"Planned Sonic Flow batch","elements":[{"elementId":"sonic-flow-batch-units-v1","name":"units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"sonic-flow-wait-seconds-v1","name":"wait_seconds","type":"real"}]}],"differentialEquations":[{"id":"sonic-flow-wait-dynamics-v1","name":"Planned batch waiting age","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","code":"return tokens.map(() => ({ wait_seconds: 1 }));"}],"parameters":[{"id":"flowbind-reorder-v1","name":"Flowbind reorder point (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_reorder_point","type":"integer","defaultValue":"1500"},{"id":"flowbind-target-v1","name":"Flowbind target (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_target","type":"integer","defaultValue":"5000"},{"id":"indian-moq-v1","name":"Indian Supplier Flowbind MOQ","variableName":"indian_moq","type":"integer","defaultValue":"2500"},{"id":"german-bridge-threshold-v1","name":"German bridge released-stock threshold","variableName":"german_bridge_threshold","type":"integer","defaultValue":"500"},{"id":"german-bridge-size-v1","name":"German Flowbind top-up (approximate)","variableName":"german_bridge_units","type":"integer","defaultValue":"250"}],"metrics":[{"id":"metric-flowbind-released-units-v1","name":"Usable Flowbind units","description":"Current quality-released Flowbind stock in standard units; a reported state measure, not fill rate or production service.","code":"const balance = state.places.FlowbindBalance.tokens;\nreturn balance.length === 0 ? 0 : balance[0].released_units;"}]}}}

timing is not yet generated.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","name":"FlowbindInQuarantine","description":"Full received Flowbind lots excluded from usable stock and from inventory position until quality disposition.","colorId":"flowbind-order-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","name":"FlowbindQualityCleared","description":"Externally supplied matching signal that quality cleared the entire quarantined lot; its replenishment and quality processes are outside this partial net.","colorId":"sonaflozin-stock-type-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null},{"id":"sonic-flow-planned-v1","name":"SonicFlowPlannedBatches","description":"Externally supplied batches entered at their original due start, with wait_seconds initially zero. Waiting age grows until enough of both materials permits immediate full start; planning schedule is not generated.","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","dynamicsEnabled":true,"differentialEquationId":"sonic-flow-wait-dynamics-v1"},{"id":"sonic-flow-in-progress-v1","name":"SonicFlowInProgress","description":"Batches started after consuming both full material quantities; completion duration and finished output are not yet represented.","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","dynamicsEnabled":false,"differentialEquationId":null}],"transitions":[{"id":"place-indian-flowbind-order-v1","name":"Place Indian Flowbind replenishment order","description":"On external review when Indian Supplier accepts orders, create a PO to the approximate target at Indian MOQ multiples if inventory position is below approximate reorder point. Later physical receipt requires a matching external arrival event; lead time is not generated.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nreturn position < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point && parameters.indian_moq > 0 && parameters.flowbind_target > position;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst position = balance.released_units + balance.open_units;\nconst amount = Math.ceil((parameters.flowbind_target - position) / parameters.indian_moq) * parameters.indian_moq;\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units + amount }],\n  IndianOpenFlowbindOrders: [{ units: amount }]\n};"},{"id":"flowbind-review-no-order-v1","name":"Complete Flowbind review without replenishment","description":"Consumes a review event when the inventory position is at or above the reorder point; leaves balances untouched.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units >= parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"flowbind-indian-unavailable-v1","name":"Indian Supplier cannot accept Flowbind order","description":"On a due review below reorder point, if Indian Supplier cannot accept orders the review ends without an Indian PO. The distinct urgent German request may occur separately on a below-500 monitoring event; acceptance and delivery are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-review-due-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn balance.released_units + balance.open_units < parameters.flowbind_reorder_point;","transitionKernelCode":"const balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"request-german-bridge-v1","name":"Request urgent German Flowbind bridge","description":"Consumes externally supplied urgent monitoring event when released stock is below 500 and Indian Supplier cannot accept an order. Emits a request only; acceptance and supply are unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-urgent-event-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"read"},{"placeId":"indian-accepting-v1","weight":1,"type":"inhibitor"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"german-bridge-request-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"return input.FlowbindBalance[0].released_units < parameters.german_bridge_threshold && parameters.german_bridge_units > 0;","transitionKernelCode":"return { GermanFlowbindTopupRequests: [{ units: parameters.german_bridge_units }] };"},{"id":"receive-indian-flowbind-v1","name":"Receive full Indian Flowbind order into quarantine","description":"On matched external physical-arrival event, closes one full Indian PO and removes its quantity from inventory position; preserves full lot in quarantine without increasing usable stock.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"indian-open-flowbind-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"indian-flowbind-arrival-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst arrival = input.IndianFlowbindArrival[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn po.order_id === arrival.order_id && po.units === arrival.units && balance.open_units >= po.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const po = input.IndianOpenFlowbindOrders[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units, open_units: balance.open_units - po.units }],\n  FlowbindInQuarantine: [{ units: po.units, order_id: po.order_id }]\n};"},{"id":"release-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Release full Flowbind lot after quality clearance","description":"On matching external quality-clearance result, consumes the entire quarantined lot and adds all its units to released usable stock in the planning balance.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-cleared-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityCleared[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst balance = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nreturn { FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: balance.released_units + lot.units, open_units: balance.open_units }] };"},{"id":"reject-flowbind-quality-v1","name":"Reject and dispose of full Flowbind lot","description":"On matching external failed quality result, consumes the whole quarantined lot and records its quantity as disposed, not released or returned to supplier.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-quarantine-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"flowbind-quality-failed-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-disposed-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nconst result = input.FlowbindQualityRejected[0];\nreturn lot.order_id === result.order_id && lot.units === result.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const lot = input.FlowbindInQuarantine[0];\nreturn { FlowbindDisposed: [{ units: lot.units, order_id: lot.order_id }] };"},{"id":"start-sonic-flow-v1","name":"Start full Sonic Flow production batch","description":"As soon as both full usable material quantities are available, starts an externally due batch, consumes one unit of each per Sonic Flow unit, and carries its planned-to-actual-start waiting age into in-progress state. Step resolution, production completion and output remain unmodelled.","inputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"sonaflozin-usable-stock-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"},{"placeId":"sonic-flow-planned-v1","weight":1,"type":"standard"}],"outputArcs":[{"placeId":"flowbind-balance-place-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"sonaflozin-usable-stock-v1","weight":1},{"placeId":"sonic-flow-in-progress-v1","weight":1}],"lambdaType":"predicate","lambdaCode":"const batch = input.SonicFlowPlannedBatches[0];\nconst flowbind = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst sonaflozin = input.SonaflozinUsableStock[0];\nreturn batch.units > 0 && flowbind.released_units >= batch.units && sonaflozin.usable_units >= batch.units;","transitionKernelCode":"const batch = input.SonicFlowPlannedBatches[0];\nconst flowbind = input.FlowbindBalance[0];\nconst sonaflozin = input.SonaflozinUsableStock[0];\nreturn {\n  FlowbindBalance: [{ released_units: flowbind.released_units - batch.units, open_units: flowbind.open_units }],\n  SonaflozinUsableStock: [{ usable_units: sonaflozin.usable_units - batch.units }],\n  SonicFlowInProgress: [{ units: batch.units, wait_seconds: batch.wait_seconds }]\n};"}],"types":[{"id":"flowbind-balance-v1","name":"Flowbind planning balance","description":"Released usable stock and open-order quantity in consistent standard units; quarantine is excluded.","elements":[{"elementId":"released-units-v1","name":"released_units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"open-units-v1","name":"open_units","type":"integer"}]},{"id":"flowbind-order-v1","name":"Flowbind purchase order","description":"One Flowbind order or received lot in standard units, with an identity for matching order, arrival and quarantine; German request is not yet an accepted order.","elements":[{"elementId":"ordered-units-v1","name":"units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"flowbind-order-identity-v1","name":"order_id","type":"uuid"}]},{"id":"sonaflozin-stock-type-v1","name":"Sonaflozin usable stock","elements":[{"elementId":"sonaflozin-available-units-v1","name":"usable_units","type":"integer"}]},{"id":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","name":"Planned Sonic Flow batch","elements":[{"elementId":"sonic-flow-batch-units-v1","name":"units","type":"integer"},{"elementId":"sonic-flow-wait-seconds-v1","name":"wait_seconds","type":"real"}]}],"differentialEquations":[{"id":"sonic-flow-wait-dynamics-v1","name":"Planned batch waiting age","colorId":"sonic-flow-batch-type-v1","code":"return tokens.map(() => ({ wait_seconds: 1 }));"}],"parameters":[{"id":"flowbind-reorder-v1","name":"Flowbind reorder point (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_reorder_point","type":"integer","defaultValue":"1500"},{"id":"flowbind-target-v1","name":"Flowbind target (approximate current)","variableName":"flowbind_target","type":"integer","defaultValue":"5000"},{"id":"indian-moq-v1","name":"Indian Supplier Flowbind MOQ","variableName":"indian_moq","type":"integer","defaultValue":"2500"},{"id":"german-bridge-threshold-v1","name":"German bridge released-stock threshold","variableName":"german_bridge_threshold","type":"integer","defaultValue":"500"},{"id":"german-bridge-size-v1","name":"German Flowbind top-up (approximate)","variableName":"german_bridge_units","type":"integer","defaultValue":"250"}],"metrics":[{"id":"metric-flowbind-released-units-v1","name":"Usable Flowbind units","description":"Current quality-released Flowbind stock in standard units; a reported state measure, not fill rate or production service.","code":"const balance = state.places.FlowbindBalance.tokens;\nreturn balance.length === 0 ? 0 : balance[0].released_units;"},{"id":"metric-started-batch-delay-days-v1","name":"Started-batch total start delay (days)","description":"Sum of planned-to-actual-start waiting days for batches currently in progress; excludes still-waiting and completed batches, so it is not total service delay.","code":"return state.places.SonicFlowInProgress.tokens.reduce((total, batch) => total + batch.wait_seconds, 0) / 86400;"}]}}}
- tool ledger_commit (call_kaxX7AzmIyfHTzhRsQGy3Rv7|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7f95438887d2a3a70d65b3026dee): {"status":"recorded","commitId":"call_kaxX7AzmIyfHTzhRsQGy3Rv7|fc_073834caff3f6333016aba7f95438887d2a3a70d65b3026dee","revision":28,"notes":[{"address":"construction/n82","supersedes":"construction/n80"},{"address":"delivery/n83","supersedes":"delivery/n77"}]}

## User
Once a lot hits its expiry date it can't go into production. Any remaining units are scrapped. The shelf life clock doesn't stop during transit or quarantine—so a delayed shipment arrives with less usable life than one that came in on time.

## Assistant
For a Flowbind lot, where does the expiry date come from, and is it already known while the lot is on order or in transit?
